THE OFFICE DEFINED [claim EX28-01]. To be priest is to SERVE BEFORE ME — the received translation's constant rendering (Onkelos Exod 28) — and the fill-the-hand installation idiom is decoded as OFFER THEIR OFFERING: the office is standing audience-service, entered by sacrifice. 		—Onkelos Exod 28witnessed
Top split head mark L=etnachta (major mid-verse rest); R head mark noted in tree. maps_to this verse only.

DO FOR ME AS I DID FOR YOU [claim EX28-08]. The garments sit inside a reciprocity architecture: the spine maps each tabernacle institution to a recorded divine act of care from the exodus (Midrash Tanchuma, Tetzaveh 1) — the measure-for-measure table at institutional scale. Seated under the amended spine, auto-seat law. 		—Midrash Tanchuma, Tetzaveh 1witnessed
WITNESS_READWITNESS-READ(oracle_protocol, access_list)
= WITNESS-READ(oracle-protocol, access-list)
THE ORACLE HAS AN ACCESS LIST [claim EX28-09]. The Urim and Tummim are consulted only IN the eight garments, and only for the KING, the COURT, or one whom the COMMUNITY needs (Mishnah Yoma 7:5) — the portable judgment organ of EX28-03 carries an authorization layer: full uniform and a qualified petitioner, or no query. Seated from the Step 9 Tetzaveh exam, auto-seat law. 		—Mishnah Yoma 7:5witnessed
